{"parentUuid":"9a3914af-ccc2-4faa-840e-db6a60d36b3f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"58479281-f0fc-437a-b3c2-51154bb8cfd1","agentId":"a95165ff517d07262","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01C78iqybKMqGxjzNY3sjCJX","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SIGIL spine segment rotation — Slice 0: manifest + path-stable lockfile + split-brain-safe migration.\n2\t\n3\tSlice 0 introduces the layout (manifest.json, spine.lock, segments/) and an EXPLICIT `migrate()`, but\n4\tkeeps behavior byte-identical until then: with no manifest the store reads/writes the legacy single file\n5\tin place. These tests pin the Slice-0 invariants:\n6\t  - a legacy store is byte-identical (no manifest / segments dir created; data stays in spine.jsonl);\n7\t  - the in-process RLock is keyed on the STABLE store.path and survives a migration (invariant 14 — the\n8\t    lock `envelope.consume` shares with `append` is the same object across a rotation);\n9\t  - migrate() renames spine.jsonl → segments/seg-00000000.jsonl (O(1)) + publishes a manifest, preserving\n10\t    the chain, next_seq, and verify();\n11\t  - a STALE appender constructed before a concurrent migrate() re-resolves the active segment under the\n12\t    lock and never resurrects spine.jsonl (the split-brain the two-step migration guards against).\n13\tRun: ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_spine_rotation.py -q\n14\t\"\"\"\n15\timport os\n16\timport tempfile\n17\tfrom pathlib import Path\n18\t\n19\tfrom sigil.spine.manifest import read_manifest, SpineLayout\n20\tfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore, spine_lock\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tdef _fresh_dir(prefix: str) -> Path:\n24\t    return Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=prefix))\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef _append_n(store: SpineStore, n: int, start: int = 0) -> None:\n28\t    for i in range(start, start + n):\n29\t        store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": i})\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tdef test_legacy_store_is_byte_identical():\n33\t    \"\"\"No manifest ⇒ active target IS the legacy single file; no manifest/segments artifacts appear.\"\"\"\n34\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-legacy-\")\n35\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n36\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n37\t    _append_n(s, 4)\n38\t    assert s._active == p, \"legacy store must append/read in place at spine.jsonl\"\n39\t    assert p.exists()\n40\t    assert not (d / \"manifest.json\").exists(), \"no manifest is written until an explicit migrate()\"\n41\t    assert not (d / \"segments\").exists(), \"segments/ is not created for a legacy/fresh store\"\n42\t    ok, reason = s.verify()\n43\t    assert ok, reason\n44\t    assert [r.seq for r in s.iter_records()] == [0, 1, 2, 3]\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef test_rlock_keyed_on_stable_path_and_survives_migration():\n48\t    \"\"\"Invariant 14: the RLock is keyed on the stable store.path, so the object `append` and\n49\t    `envelope.consume` both take via spine_lock(store.path) is identical — and stays identical across a\n50\t    migration (store.path never changes, even though spine.jsonl is renamed away).\"\"\"\n51\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-rlock-\")\n52\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n53\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n54\t    _append_n(s, 2)\n55\t    lock_before = spine_lock(s.path)\n56\t    assert spine_lock(s.path) is lock_before, \"same path ⇒ same RLock object\"\n57\t    assert s.migrate() is True\n58\t    assert s.path == p, \"store.path (the lock/identity token) is unchanged by migration\"\n59\t    assert spine_lock(s.path) is lock_before, \"the RLock survives the migration unchanged\"\n60\t\n61\t\n62\tdef test_migrate_preserves_chain_and_moves_to_segment():\n63\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-mig-\")\n64\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n65\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n66\t    _append_n(s, 5)\n67\t    before = [(r.seq, r.entry_hash, r.payload[\"n\"]) for r in s.iter_records()]\n68\t\n69\t    assert s.migrate() is True\n70\t    assert not p.exists(), \"spine.jsonl is renamed away and never a data file again\"\n71\t    assert (d / \"segments\" / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\").exists()\n72\t    assert (d / \"manifest.json\").exists()\n73\t\n74\t    # reads identical, chain intact, tip preserved\n75\t    assert [(r.seq, r.entry_hash, r.payload[\"n\"]) for r in s.iter_records()] == before\n76\t    ok, reason = s.verify()\n77\t    assert ok, reason\n78\t    assert s.next_seq == 5\n79\t\n80\t    # a FRESH instance resolves the active segment via the manifest and reads the same chain\n81\t    fresh = SpineStore(p)\n82\t    assert [r.seq for r in fresh.iter_records()] == [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]\n83\t    assert fresh.get(2) is not None and fresh.get(2).payload[\"n\"] == 2\n84\t\n85\t    # appends continue cleanly with no seq reuse\n86\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 5})\n87\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n88\t    assert ok, reason\n89\t    assert [r.seq for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()] == [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5]\n90\t\n91\t\n92\tdef test_migrate_is_idempotent():\n93\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-idem-\")\n94\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n95\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n96\t    _append_n(s, 3)\n97\t    assert s.migrate() is True\n98\t    gen1 = read_manifest(SpineLayout.for_path(p)).generation\n99\t    assert s.migrate() is False, \"a second migrate is a no-op\"\n100\t    assert read_manifest(SpineLayout.for_path(p)).generation == gen1\n101\t\n102\t\n103\tdef test_migrate_fresh_creates_empty_active():\n104\t    \"\"\"A fresh (never-appended) store migrates by creating an empty active segment; appends then work.\"\"\"\n105\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-freshmig-\")\n106\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n107\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n108\t    assert s.migrate() is True\n109\t    assert not p.exists()\n110\t    seg0 = d / \"segments\" / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\"\n111\t    assert seg0.exists() and seg0.stat().st_size == 0\n112\t    assert s.next_seq == 0\n113\t    _append_n(s, 2)\n114\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n115\t    assert ok, reason\n116\t    assert [r.seq for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()] == [0, 1]\n117\t\n118\t\n119\tdef test_orphan_migration_is_reconciled():\n120\t    \"\"\"The judge-flagged migration crash window: a crash AFTER the atomic rename but BEFORE the manifest\n121\t    write leaves seg-0 present, spine.jsonl gone, no manifest. The store MUST reconcile (finish the\n122\t    migration) and recover the records — never read the spine as empty and hide all history.\"\"\"\n123\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-orphan-\")\n124\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n125\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n126\t    _append_n(s, 4)\n127\t    before = [r.seq for r in s.iter_records()]\n128\t\n129\t    # simulate the crash: rename done, manifest NOT written\n130\t    (d / \"segments\").mkdir()\n131\t    os.replace(p, d / \"segments\" / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\")\n132\t    assert not p.exists() and (d / \"segments\" / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\").exists()\n133\t    assert not (d / \"manifest.json\").exists()\n134\t\n135\t    s2 = SpineStore(p)                                     # __init__ must reconcile\n136\t    assert (d / \"manifest.json\").exists(), \"reconciler publishes the manifest to complete the migration\"\n137\t    assert [r.seq for r in s2.iter_records()] == before, \"records recovered, NOT hidden as an empty spine\"\n138\t    ok, reason = s2.verify()\n139\t    assert ok, reason\n140\t\n141\t\n142\tdef test_stale_appender_reresolves_no_split_brain():\n143\t    \"\"\"The core split-brain test. Instance A appends in legacy mode; instance B migrates; A appends\n144\t    again. A MUST re-resolve the active segment under the lock and write to the migrated segment — never\n145\t    resurrect spine.jsonl and fork the chain.\"\"\"\n146\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-sb-\")\n147\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n148\t    a = SpineStore(p)\n149\t    a.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 0})\n150\t    assert a._active == p                                   # A cached the legacy path\n151\t\n152\t    b = SpineStore(p)                                       # \"another process\"\n153\t    assert b.migrate() is True                              # p is renamed → seg-0; A's cached _active is now stale\n154\t\n155\t    a.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 1})   # A must re-resolve under the lock\n156\t    assert not p.exists(), \"a stale appender must not resurrect spine.jsonl (split-brain / fork)\"\n157\t\n158\t    recs = [r.payload[\"n\"] for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()]\n159\t    assert recs == [0, 1], f\"both records land in the migrated segment, no fork: {recs}\"\n160\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n161\t    assert ok, reason\n162\t    seqs = [r.seq for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()]\n163\t    assert seqs == [0, 1], f\"no seq reuse across the migration seam: {seqs}\"\n164\t"}]},"uuid":"043c579c-2476-44fe-aada-757dd61ebab8","timestamp":"2026-07-19T15:54:56.068Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9a3914af-ccc2-4faa-840e-db6a60d36b3f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

O(1) `stat` when the spine is unchanged since the\n        last authoritative scan; a grown (or first-seen / shrunk) file re-runs the real scan below and\n        refreshes the shared cache. Semantics are IDENTICAL to a fresh scan — a new engage/release grows\n        the file, so it is always honored on the next call.\"\"\"\n        key = (str(Path(self.store.path).resolve()), self.trusted_pubkey)\n        try:\n            size = self.store.path.stat().st_size\n        except OSError:\n            size = -1\n        if size >= 0:\n            with _CACHE_GUARD:\n                cached = _STATE_CACHE.get(key)\n                if cached is not None and cached[0] == size:\n                    return cached[1]\n        engaged = self._scan_engaged()\n        if size >= 0:\n            with _CACHE_GUARD:\n                _STATE_CACHE[key] = (size, engaged)\n        return engaged\n\n    def _scan_engaged(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"The AUTHORITATIVE scan — unchanged semantics (not re-implemented): honor ANY engage (halting\n        is fail-safe), and un-halt only on an OWNER-SIGNED release that verifies (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n        engaged = False\n        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n            p = r.payload\n            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL:\n                continue\n            state = p.get(\"state\")\n            if state == \"engaged\":\n=== SESSION 110,135 ===\n        return self._trusted_pubkey\n\n    def _killswitch_engaged(self, now: float) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Kill-switch state for the per-intent gate. Re-scans the AUTHORITATIVE `KillSwitch` (which\n        verifies the owner-signed release — never re-implemented here) ONLY when the append-only spine\n        has GROWN since the last scan: a panic APPENDS a record → the file grows → the halt is honored\n        within ~1-2 frames (≤ the rescan floor + one frame, ≈66 ms worst case), not a fixed 0.5 s. A\n        pure-movement gesture appends nothing, so this is\n        O(1) (a `stat`) with NO scan at all. A short `_KS_MIN_RESCAN` floor stops a churning spine from\n        forcing a per-frame O(spine) scan (worst-case latency ≤ the floor). The arm path checks FRESH.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            size = self.store.path.stat().st_size\n        except OSError:\n            size = self._ks_size\n        if size != self._ks_size and (now - self._ks_checked_at) >= _KS_MIN_RESCAN:\n            from ..governor.killswitch import KillSwitch\n            self._ks_engaged = KillSwitch(self.store).is_engaged()\n            self._ks_size = size\n            self._ks_checked_at = now\n        return self._ks_engaged\n\n    def _cls(self):\n        if self._classifier is None:\n            from ..agents.kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n        return self._classifier\n=== ENVELOPE 120,145 ===\n    try:\n        nonce = int(n_raw)                                 # int(inf) raises OverflowError (NOT a ValueError) — catch it\n    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):\n        raise ValueError(\"invalid nonce\")\n    if nonce < 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"invalid nonce\")\n    if effectful:\n        # ATOMIC check-then-receipt: hold the re-entrant spine lock across the highwater read AND the\n        # receipt append, so two concurrent replays of one captured envelope can't both pass (TOCTOU).\n        from ..spine.store import spine_lock\n        with spine_lock(store.path):\n            if nonce <= device_nonce_highwater(store, core[\"device\"]):\n                raise ValueError(\"replay: nonce not fresh\")\n            record_receipt(store, core)\n    else:\n        record_receipt(store, core)\n    return core","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"46109e85-0a7a-4026-9aa2-e2f7b9a4bd8d","timestamp":"2026-07-19T15:55:21.492Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"81994f52-04e1-4cfa-98db-4a39a6886461","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

But the layout-derivation design breaks store isolation in a way that is already demonstrable against the existing test suite, and `migrate()` — the one new capability this slice ships — silently defeats the kill-switch freshness path. Two BLOCK-class findings, one MEDIUM, and notes.\n\n## BLOCK-1 — `SpineLayout` roots manifest/lockfile/segments in `data_path.parent`, which is NOT store-private; cross-store contamination that demonstrably breaks 15+ existing tests\n\n`manifest.py:89-96` (`SpineLayout.for_path`) derives `manifest_path`, `lockfile_path`, `segments_dir`, `trash_dir` from `Path(data_path).parent`. The test suite constructs stores as `SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))` in **at least 10 modules** (test_scrape, test_cli_mesh, test_gesture_remote, test_vision, test_operator, test_gesture_arm, test_shared_primitives, test_consolidate, test_bridge_recall, test_hardening). `tempfile.mktemp()` returns a **bare file in `/tmp`**, so every one of those stores derives the SAME `/tmp/manifest.json`, `/tmp/spine.lock`, `/tmp/segments/`.\n\nGrounded repro (ran it):\n- Store A at `/tmp/tmpA.jsonl`, 3 records, `A.migrate()` → writes `/tmp/manifest.json` + `/tmp/segments/seg-00000000.jsonl`.\n- Store B constructed at a **different** `/tmp/tmpB.jsonl` → `__init__` reads A's `/tmp/manifest.json`, `_resolve_active_path` (`store.py:169-172`) → `seg_path` (`manifest.py:98-100`) resolves `_active` to **A's segment**. B \"sees\" A's 3 records before ever appending, and B's first `append` writes seq 3 **into A's segment**. B's own data file is never created.\n- With that stray `/tmp/manifest.json` present I ran `pytest tests/test_hardening.py tests/test_consolidate.py` → **15+ failures**; deleting it → all green. So a single stray manifest (left by any dev/interrupted run that migrated a bare-path store) silently poisons the suite — including making tests read foreign data.\n\nSecond, migrate-free vector: `_reconcile_orphan_migration` (`store.py:221-240`) runs in **every** `__init__`; a fresh bare-`/tmp` store, if `/tmp/segments/seg-00000000.jsonl` exists and its own path doesn't, **adopts the foreign segment and writes `/tmp/manifest.json`** (\"completing an interrupted migration\") — foreign data becomes its spine.\n\nThe module docstrings assert the opposite — \"a store on a temp path is fully isolated\" (`manifest.py:12-13`, `80-81`). That invariant is **false** for co-located stores. The committed suite is green today only because the sole `migrate()` caller (test_spine_rotation) happens to use `mkdtemp`; nothing in the design enforces that. Fix direction: namespace by the data-file stem (`<stem>.manifest.json`, `<stem>.segments/`, `<stem>.lock`) or make the store own its directory and switch the bare-`mktemp` tests to `mkdtemp`. The code must not assume `data_path.parent` is private.\n\n## BLOCK-2 — After `migrate()`, `store.path` disappears and the kill-switch freshness token freezes (safety, un-halting direction)\n\nTwo consumers use `store.path.stat().st_size` as their kill-switch change-token: `governor/killswitch.py:57` and `gesture/session.py:121`. `migrate()` renames `spine.jsonl` away (`store.py:260`), so `store.path.stat()` raises `OSError` forever after.\n\n- `gesture/session.py:112-130`: `except OSError: size = self._ks_size` → the rescan guard `size != self._ks_size` **never fires** → it returns the stale `self._ks_engaged` indefinitely. Grounded repro: the token is frozen at `None` across the migrate AND across a subsequent panic append. Consequence: a kill-switch **PANIC engaged after an operator runs migrate() is never observed by an already-running gesture session** — it keeps executing gestures with a halt outstanding.\n- `governor/killswitch.py:55-70`: `OSError → size=-1` → cache disabled → full O(spine) scan every `is_engaged()` (perf regression; correctness intact).\n\nThis is exactly hazard **A4** in the design brief (\"a same-size rotation serves a STALE kill-switch verdict in the un-halting direction … fix must be UNIFIED (shared epoch token)\"). Slice 0 added `Manifest.generation` as the intended unified token but **did not rewire either consumer to it**. It's gated on `migrate()` being called — which is the entire purpose of the slice — so shipping migrate() without fixing these two sites ships the un-halting regression. (The default un-migrated deployment is unaffected today, which is why it's BLOCK-on-the-new-capability rather than a live prod break.)\n\n## MEDIUM-1 — `seg.file` path-escape; reads are not fail-closed for `get()/iter/tail`\n\n`manifest.py:98-100` (`seg_path`) is `spine_dir / seg.file` with no validation, and `Segment.file` is `extra=\"ignore\"`/unvalidated. Confirmed: `file=\"/etc/hostname\"` → `/etc/hostname`; `file=\"../../../etc/hostname\"` escapes the spine dir. The module claims a doctored manifest \"fails CLOSED\" (`manifest.py:5-10`) — true for `verify()/count()` (byte re-derivation), but `get()/iter_records()/tail()` don't run verify and would return arbitrary/foreign file content **as spine records**. Same-trust domain (writing the manifest implies writing segments), so not privilege escalation, but it breaks the fail-closed/isolation claim and compounds BLOCK-1. Reject non-relative or `..`-escaping `file`.\n\n## Notes (works, but worth flagging)\n\n- **N1 — dir not byte-identical (parity nit):** legacy append now creates `spine.lock` in the spine dir (`store.py:184-185` via `_crossproc_lock`). The data file is byte-identical, but the headline \"byte-identical\" is data-file-only. `cli.py:22` (`SPINE_PATH.unlink(missing_ok=True)`) removes only `spine.jsonl`, leaving `spine.lock` residue.\n- **N2 — new per-append cost:** `_refresh_active_under_lock` (`store.py:195-210`) calls `read_manifest` on **every** append — a `stat` in legacy, a full read + pydantic parse once migrated. Acknowledged as Slice-1-deferred, but until then every append re-parses the manifest.\n- **N3 — latent seq-reuse for rotation:** `_read_last_entry` (`store.py:436-443`) reads the tip from `self._active` only. Fine for Slice 0 (seg-0 holds all history, incl. after migrate). But when rotation (Slice 3) creates a new **empty** active segment, `_read_last_entry` returns `None` → next append does `build_chain(...)` → **seq 0 reuse** (invariants 3/13). The current single-segment shape of `_read_last_entry`/`next_seq` must go segment-spanning before rotation lands.\n- **N4 — parity CONFIRMED (not a defect):** with no manifest, `_resolve_active_path`→`self.path`, `_reconcile_orphan_migration` early-returns, and `_refresh_active_under_lock`'s condition (`store.py:206`) is always False → no spurious index invalidation and no `_last` reset. `iter_records/get/tail/count/entries/verify/_read_last_entry/_ensure_index/_scan_from` only swapped `self.path`→`self._active`(==`self.path`); append still derives the tip from `_last_valid_boundary(active)` with unchanged record shape / cert_digest / fsync-before-ack. Full suite green (355 passed).\n- **N5 — next_seq after migrate CONFIRMED correct:** 5 records → `next_seq==5`; fresh migrate → `next_seq==0`. No reset/reuse in Slice 0.\n\n**Bottom line:** the byte-identical claim for the data file holds, but \"fully isolated layout\" is false (BLOCK-1, demonstrated), and `migrate()` ships an un-halting kill-switch regression its own design brief predicted (BLOCK-2). Both must be fixed before merge."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":168,"cache_read_input_tokens":97324,"output_tokens":5978,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":168},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":5978,"cache_read_input_tokens":97324,"cache_creation_input_tokens":168,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":168,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdBhN95sxxjrEFSzy4c2C","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"b01847b8-a6de-4948-a332-b6ce03403a18","timestamp":"2026-07-19T16:03:03.240Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
